return(function()local J38=math.floor local RU=math.ldexp local Mey=table.unpack or unpack local MDH=table.pack or function(...)return{n=select('#',...),...}end local IU=(getfenv and getfenv(1))or _ENV or _G local R4,XPv,sB if bit32 then R4,XPv,sB=bit32.band,bit32.rshift,bit32.bxor else R4=function(x,y)local r,p=0,1 for _=1,32 do local rx,ry=x%2,y%2 if rx==1 and ry==1 then r=r+p end x=J38(x/2);y=J38(y/2);p=p*2 end return r end XPv=function(x,n)return J38(x/(2^n))%4294967296 end sB=function(x,y)local r,p=0,1 for _=1,32 do local rx,ry=x%2,y%2 if rx~=ry then r=r+p end x=J38(x/2);y=J38(y/2);p=p*2 end return r end end local function Ot(b85)if b85=="0"then return{}end local alpha="!#$%&()*+,-./0123456789:;<=>?@ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"local map={}for i=1,85 do map[string.byte(alpha,i)]=i-1 end local pad=string.byte(b85,1)-48 local len=#b85 local raw={}local n=0 for i=2,len,5 do local b1,b2,b3,b4,b5=string.byte(b85,i,i+4)local val=map[b1]*52200625+map[b2]*614125+map[b3]*7225+map[b4]*85+map[b5]raw[n+1]=J38(val/16777216)%256 raw[n+2]=J38(val/65536)%256 raw[n+3]=J38(val/256)%256 raw[n+4]=val%256 n=n+4 end for _=1,pad do raw[n]=nil;n=n-1 end local state=((915812+637059)+1460770130)local mul=((444692+223086)-126807)local inc=((975036+566492)+1795833831)local skey=((792952+766388)-1559249)for i=1,n do state=(state*mul+inc)%4294967296 raw[i]=sB(raw[i],J38(state/16777216))end if n<5 then return{}end local body_len=n-4 local chk=2166136261 for i=1,body_len do chk=(sB(chk,raw[i])*65521+97)%4294967296 end local exp_chk=raw[body_len+1]+raw[body_len+2]*256+raw[body_len+3]*65536+raw[body_len+4]*16777216 if chk~=exp_chk then return{}end local pos=1 local comp_flag=raw[pos];pos=pos+1 if comp_flag==1 then local uncomp_len=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256+raw[pos+2]*65536+raw[pos+3]*16777216 pos=pos+4 local lzout={}local op_pos=0 local ip=pos while ip<=body_len do local tok=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 local lit_len=J38(tok/16)if lit_len==15 then while ip<=body_len do local sb=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 lit_len=lit_len+sb if sb~=255 then break end end end for _=1,lit_len do op_pos=op_pos+1;lzout[op_pos]=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 end if ip>body_len then break end local off=raw[ip]+raw[ip+1]*256;ip=ip+2 local mlen=(tok%16)+4 if(tok%16)==15 then while ip<=body_len do local sb=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 mlen=mlen+sb if sb~=255 then break end end end local mpos=op_pos-off for _=1,mlen do op_pos=op_pos+1;mpos=mpos+1 lzout[op_pos]=lzout[mpos]end end if op_pos~=uncomp_len then return{}end raw=lzout pos=1 end local function ru8()local x=raw[pos];pos=pos+1;return x end local function ru16()local x=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256;pos=pos+2;return x end local function ru32()local x=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256+raw[pos+2]*65536+raw[pos+3]*16777216 pos=pos+4;return x end local function ri32()local x=ru32()if x>=2147483648 then x=x-4294967296 end return x end local function rf64()local b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6,b7,b8=raw[pos],raw[pos+1],raw[pos+2],raw[pos+3],raw[pos+4],raw[pos+5],raw[pos+6],raw[pos+7]pos=pos+8 local lo=b1+b2*256+b3*65536+b4*16777216 local sign=(b8>=128)and-1 or 1 local exp=(b8%128)*16+J38(b7/16)local top20=(b7%16)*65536+b6*256+b5 local mant=top20*4294967296+lo if exp==0 then if mant==0 then return sign*0 end return sign*RU(mant,-1074)elseif exp==2047 then return(mant==0)and(sign*math.huge)or(0/0)end return sign*RU(mant+4503599627370496,exp-1075)end local function rstr(slen)if slen==0 then return""end local chunks={}local ci=0 local stop=pos+slen-1 for s=pos,stop,1024 do local e=s+1023 if e>stop then e=stop end ci=ci+1 chunks[ci]=string.char(Mey(raw,s,e))end pos=stop+1 return(ci==1)and chunks[1]or table.concat(chunks)end local num_protos=ru32()local plist={}for pi=1,num_protos do local nparams=ru8()local is_va=ru8()~=0 local max_stk=ru8()local nuv=ru16()local uvs={}for i=1,nuv do uvs[i]=ru16()end local nk=ru32()local ks={}local kenc={}local has_kenc=false for i=1,nk do local tag=ru8()if tag==0 then ks[i]=nil elseif tag==1 then ks[i]=(ru8()~=0)elseif tag==2 then ks[i]=ri32()elseif tag==3 then ks[i]=rf64()else ks[i]=rstr(ru32())if#ks[i]>0 then kenc[i]=true;has_kenc=true end end end local nch=ru16()local ch={}for i=1,nch do ch[i]=ru16()+1 end local ncode=ru32()local code={}for i=1,ncode do code[i]=ru32()end plist[pi]={nparams,is_va,max_stk,uvs,ks,ch,code,has_kenc and kenc or nil,pi}end return plist end local
